Headless (`claude -p`) layer that executes the SAFE, no-merge tier of a pipeline tick plan — route-foundational, drain-answer, drain-parse-miss, drain-clarification, retro-judge, and kind:spike drives — by invoking the real pipeline commands. STRUCTURALLY cannot merge: it is handed only the pre-filtered safe actions and is forbidden to open PRs, merge, or drive a kind:code item. Spawned by pipeline-drive.sh; the merging tier waits for level 5c. ([Towheads/temperloop_legacy](https://github.com/Towheads/temperloop_legacy/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/pipeline-drive.md))
- **pipeline-drive-merge**: Level 5c EXECUTOR of the autonomous pipeline driver — the MERGING tier. Headless (`claude -p`) layer that auto-executes the kind:code drives 5b deliberately leaves for the operator, by driving each through `/build --unattended` and letting /build's OWN timed/modal merge gate decide the merge. Spawned by pipeline-drive.sh under a merge-allowing containment overlay, gated by PIPELINE_DRIVE_MERGE=1 and a per-tick cap. It merges ONLY via /build's gated path — never by hand. ([Towheads/temperloop_legacy](https://github.com/Towheads/temperloop_legacy/blob/HEAD/claude/commands/pipeline-drive-merge.md))

- **pipeline-undo**: Undo a pipeline run's result. "Undo" therefore means deleting that branch and its worktree, not reverting your working tree. - **Plan**: End-to-end planning in one command — decides whether to attach to an existing epic or create a new one (reading the live board once), plans and drafts multiple stories, then writes expert-level tasks per story (architecture, DB schema, API contract, FE design, security, performance, failure modes) grounded in the real code. Five gates — epic decision, story plan, story drafts, task drafts, publish. A deterministic quality gate rejects incomplete plans; publishing to the PM tool is idempotent (re-runs update, never duplicate). Builds a project knowledge base in .evyasys/knowledge/.
